	{

			{
				return;

			entity.height -= 5;
			entity.position.Y += 5f;
		}

		int num3 = (int)entity.position.Y + entity.height;
		entity.Hitbox.Inflate(2, 2);
		_ = entity.TopLeft;
		_ = entity.TopRight;

		_ = entity.BottomRight;
		List<Point> cacheForConveyorBelts = _cacheForConveyorBelts;
		cacheForConveyorBelts.Clear();
		GetEntityEdgeTiles(cacheForConveyorBelts, entity, left: false, right: false);

	public static bool TryFindingConveyorBeltRising(Entity entity, float gravDir)
	{
		if (entity is WorldItem)
		{
			Point p = (entity.BottomLeft + new Vector2(-0.5f, -0.01f)).ToTileCoordinates();
			float num = 0f;
			if (WorldGen.InWorld(p))
			{
				Tile tile = Main.tile[p.X, p.Y];
				if (tile != null && tile.active() && tile.nactive() && tile.slope() == 0)
				{
					int num2 = TileID.Sets.ConveyorDirection[tile.type];
					num += (float)num2 * gravDir;
				}
			}
			Point p2 = (entity.BottomRight + new Vector2(0.5f, -0.01f)).ToTileCoordinates();
			if (WorldGen.InWorld(p2))
			{
				Tile tile2 = Main.tile[p2.X, p2.Y];
				if (tile2 != null && tile2.active() && tile2.nactive() && tile2.slope() == 0)
				{
					int num3 = TileID.Sets.ConveyorDirection[tile2.type];
					num += (float)num3 * gravDir * -1f;
				}
			}
			return num < 0f;
		}
		return false;
	}

	{

		int num2 = Utils.Clamp(point.Y, 0, Main.maxTilesY - 40);
		int num3 = Utils.Clamp(point2.X, 0, Main.maxTilesX - 1);
		int num4 = Utils.Clamp(point2.Y, 0, Main.maxTilesY - 40);
		for (int i = num; i <= num3; i++)
		{
			for (int j = num2; j <= num4; j++)
			{
				if (Main.tile[i, j] != null)
				{
					list.Add(new Point(i, j));
				}
			}
		}
		return list;

	public static void GetEntityEdgeTiles(List<Point> p, Entity entity, bool left = true, bool right = true, bool up = true, bool down = true)
	{
		int num = (int)entity.position.X;
		int num2 = (int)entity.position.Y;
		_ = num % 16;
		_ = num2 % 16;
		int num3 = (int)entity.Right.X;
		int num4 = (int)entity.Bottom.Y;
		if (num % 16 == 0)
		{
			num--;
		}
		if (num2 % 16 == 0)
		{
			num2--;
		}
		if (num3 % 16 == 0)
		{
			num3++;
		}
		if (num4 % 16 == 0)
		{
			num4++;
		}
		int num5 = num3 / 16 - num / 16;
		int num6 = num4 / 16 - num2 / 16;
		num /= 16;
		num2 /= 16;
		for (int i = num; i <= num + num5; i++)
		{
			if (up)
			{
				p.Add(new Point(i, num2));
			}
			if (down)
			{
				p.Add(new Point(i, num2 + num6));
			}
		}
		for (int j = num2; j < num2 + num6; j++)
		{
			if (left)
			{
				p.Add(new Point(num, j));
			}
			if (right)
			{
				p.Add(new Point(num + num5, j));
			}
		}
	}

	public static bool CheckAABBvLineCollision(Vector2 objectPosition, Vector2 objectDimensions, Vector2 lineStart, Vector2 lineEnd, float lineWidth, ref float collisionPoint)
	{
		float num = lineWidth * 0.5f;
		Vector2 position = lineStart;
		Vector2 dimensions = lineEnd - lineStart;
		if (dimensions.X > 0f)
		{
			dimensions.X += lineWidth;
			position.X -= num;
		}
		else
		{
			position.X += dimensions.X - num;
			dimensions.X = 0f - dimensions.X + lineWidth;
		}
		if (dimensions.Y > 0f)
		{
			dimensions.Y += lineWidth;
			position.Y -= num;
		}
		else
		{
			position.Y += dimensions.Y - num;
			dimensions.Y = 0f - dimensions.Y + lineWidth;

		Vector2 spinningpoint3 = new Vector2(spinningpoint.X, vector.Y);
		Vector2 vector2 = lineEnd - lineStart;
		float num2 = vector2.Length();

			spinningpoint3.RotatedBy(0f - num3),
			spinningpoint.RotatedBy(0f - num3),
			spinningpoint2.RotatedBy(0f - num3)
		};
		collisionPoint = num2;

		for (int i = 0; i < array.Length; i++)

				collisionPoint = array[i].X;
				result = true;
			}
		}

	public static bool RectangleLineCollision(Vector2 rectTopLeft, Vector2 rectBottomRight, Vector2 lineStart, Vector2 lineEnd)
	{
		if (lineStart.Between(rectTopLeft, rectBottomRight) || lineEnd.Between(rectTopLeft, rectBottomRight))
		{
			return true;
		}
		Vector2 p = new Vector2(rectBottomRight.X, rectTopLeft.Y);
		Vector2 vector = new Vector2(rectTopLeft.X, rectBottomRight.Y);
		Vector2[] array = new Vector2[4]
		{
			rectTopLeft.ClosestPointOnLine(lineStart, lineEnd),
			p.ClosestPointOnLine(lineStart, lineEnd),
			vector.ClosestPointOnLine(lineStart, lineEnd),
			rectBottomRight.ClosestPointOnLine(lineStart, lineEnd)
		};
		for (int i = 0; i < array.Length; i++)
		{
			if (array[0].Between(rectTopLeft, vector))
			{
				return true;
			}
		}
		return false;
	}

	public static Vector2 RotateRandom(this Vector2 spinninpoint, double maxRadians)
	{
		return spinninpoint.RotatedBy(Main.rand.NextDouble() * maxRadians - Main.rand.NextDouble() * maxRadians);
	}

	public static float AngleTo(this Vector2 Origin, Vector2 Target)

6	export abstract class Entity {
7	  id = 0;
8	  x = 0; y = 0;          // 左上角 px
9	  w = 16; h = 16;
10	  vx = 0; vy = 0;
11	  dead = false;

17	  netPuppet = false;
18	  /** 联机实体号（拥有者 slot 高 8 位 + 本地 id 低 24 位；见 net/entitySyncHooks） */
19	  netId = 0;
20	
21	  abstract fixedUpdate(dt: number, game: GameHooks): void;
22	  abstract draw(r: Renderer, cam: Camera): void;
23	
24	  get cx() { return this.x + this.w / 2; }
25	  get cy() { return this.y + this.h / 2; }
26	  aabbOverlaps(o: Entity): boolean {
27	    return this.x < o.x + o.w && this.x + this.w > o.x && this.y < o.y + o.h && this.y + this.h > o.y;
28	  }
29	}
30	
31	/** 就地剔除 dead 实体(保持顺序,零分配) */
32	function compact(list: Entity[]): void {
33	  let w = 0;
34	  for (let r = 0; r < list.length; r++) {
35	    const e = list[r];
36	    if (!e.dead) list[w++] = e;
37	  }
38	  list.length = w;
39	}
40	
41	export class EntityManager {
42	  nextId = 1;
43	  enemies: Entity[] = [];
44	  drops: Entity[] = [];
45	  npcs: Entity[] = [];
46	  projectiles: Entity[] = [];
47	  props: Entity[] = [];   // 场景物件（墓碑等不可拾取物理实体）
48	  critters: Entity[] = []; // 小动物（无害被动生物）
49	
50	  add(e: Entity, bucket: 'enemies' | 'drops' | 'npcs' | 'projectiles' | 'props' | 'critters') {
51	    e.id = this.nextId++;
52	    this[bucket].push(e);
53	  }
54	
55	  update(dt: number, game: GameHooks) {
56	    // 就地压缩替代 6×filter(2026-08 审计 G9:每 tick 6 个新数组,60Hz 恒定分配)
57	    for (const list of [this.enemies, this.drops, this.npcs, this.projectiles, this.props, this.critters]) {
58	      for (const e of list) e.fixedUpdate(dt, game);
59	      compact(list);
60	    }
61	  }
62	
63	  clear() {
64	    this.enemies.length = 0;
65	    this.drops.length = 0;
66	    this.npcs.length = 0;
67	    this.projectiles.length = 0;
68	    this.props.length = 0;
69	    this.critters.length = 0;
70	  }
71	
72	  all(): Entity[] {
73	    return [...this.enemies, ...this.drops, ...this.npcs, ...this.projectiles, ...this.props, ...this.critters];
74	  }
75	}

// SoA 多通道平面数组存储。所有热点代码只碰 TypedArray。
import { TILE_DEFS } from '../data/tiles';
import { TileOpAction } from '../net/protocol';

export class TileStore {
  readonly w: number;
  readonly h: number;
  readonly type: Uint16Array;
  readonly flags: Uint8Array; // bit0 active bit1 framedDecor占位(预留)
  readonly frameX: Uint16Array;
  readonly frameY: Uint16Array;
  readonly wall: Uint16Array; // 原版 wld 墙 id（0-366）
  readonly liquid: Uint8Array;   // 0-255 液量
  readonly liquidType: Uint8Array; // 0无 1水 2岩浆 3蜂蜜 4微光(原版 liquidType 3,本仓库 +1 编码;任意液体遇微光 → Aetherium 块 659)
  readonly half: Uint8Array;      // halfBrick 位（原版 Tile.cs bTileHeader bit1）：下半 8px 实心
  readonly slope: Uint8Array;     // 坡面 0-4（原版编码；渲染暂未支持，生成期少用）
  readonly wire: Uint8Array;      // 电路位（原版 Tile.cs sTileHeader）：bit0红 bit1蓝 bit2绿 bit3黄 bit4致动器 bit5已致动(inActive)
  // wire 位掩码常量（对应原版 Tile.cs:396-459 的 wire/wire2/wire3/wire4/actuator/inActive）
  static readonly WIRE_RED = 1;
  static readonly WIRE_BLUE = 2;
  static readonly WIRE_GREEN = 4;
  static readonly WIRE_YELLOW = 8;
  static readonly WIRE_ACTUATOR = 16;
  static readonly WIRE_ACTUATED = 32;
  static readonly WIRE_COLORS = 15; // 四色掩码

  // tile 变更监听（chunk 缓存 / 光照脏标记 / 液体模拟统一从这里拿事件）
  private listeners: Array<(x: number, y: number) => void> = [];

  // ---- 多人联机上报钩子（ClientNet 注入；单机为 null 零开销） ----
  /** 本地 tile 变更上报（协议层 TileOp 收集器）。liquid 通道不报（客户端本地模拟） */
  netReporter: ((op: import('../net/protocol').TileOp) => void) | null = null;
  /** 应用远端操作期间置位：上报钩子静默（防回环） */
  netSuppress = false;

  constructor(w: number, h: number, bufs?: import('../workers/protocol').TileBuffers) {
    this.w = w; this.h = h;
    // buffers 注入（worker transfer 重建路径）：直接以移交的 ArrayBuffer 包装 TypedArray，
    // 跳过分配与拷贝。缺省 explored 由调用方（World.fromPacket）补齐
    if (bufs) {
      this.type = new Uint16Array(bufs.type);
      this.flags = new Uint8Array(bufs.flags);
      this.frameX = new Uint16Array(bufs.frameX);
      this.frameY = new Uint16Array(bufs.frameY);
      this.wall = new Uint16Array(bufs.wall);
      this.liquid = new Uint8Array(bufs.liquid);
      this.liquidType = new Uint8Array(bufs.liquidType);
      this.half = new Uint8Array(bufs.half);
      this.slope = new Uint8Array(bufs.slope);
      this.wire = new Uint8Array(bufs.wire);
      return;
    }
    const n = w * h;
    this.type = new Uint16Array(n);
    this.flags = new Uint8Array(n);
    this.frameX = new Uint16Array(n);
    this.frameY = new Uint16Array(n);
    this.wall = new Uint16Array(n);
    this.liquid = new Uint8Array(n);
    this.liquidType = new Uint8Array(n);
    this.half = new Uint8Array(n);
    this.slope = new Uint8Array(n);
    this.wire = new Uint8Array(n);
  }

  idx(x: number, y: number): number { return y * this.w + x; }

  inBounds(x: number, y: number): boolean {
    return x >= 0 && y >= 0 && x < this.w && y < this.h;
  }

  get(x: number, y: number): number { return this.inBounds(x, y) ? this.type[this.idx(x, y)] : 0; }

  isActive(x: number, y: number): boolean {
    // 原版 nactive() = active && !inActive（Tile.cs:383-394）：已致动格视为不活跃
    return this.inBounds(x, y) && this.type[this.idx(x, y)] !== 0
      && (this.wire[this.idx(x, y)] & TileStore.WIRE_ACTUATED) === 0;
  }

  /** 原版 inActive()：致动器翻转后的"缩入地下"态（有 type 但不碰撞不阻挡） */

```

1	// 分轴扫描 tile AABB 碰撞
2	import { TILE } from '../core/constants';
3	import type { World } from '../world/World';
4	
5	export interface Body {
6	  x: number; y: number;       // 左上角（px）
7	  w: number; h: number;
8	  vx: number; vy: number;
9	  onGround: boolean;
10	  hitWall: boolean;
11	  hitHead: boolean;
12	  /** 平台单向碰撞：仅当开启且下落时与平台碰撞 */
13	  dropThrough?: boolean;
14	}
15	
16	/** 格子的半砖碰撞盒（原版 Collision.cs:1320-1324 三处一致）：下半 8px。
17	 *  返回 null = 非实心；[top, bottom] = 碰撞盒的像素 y 区间 */
18	function solidSpan(world: World, tx: number, ty: number): [number, number] | null {
19	  const st = world.store;
20	  if (!st.isSolid(tx, ty)) return null;
21	  const top = ty * TILE + (st.half[st.idx(tx, ty)] ? 8 : 0);
22	  return [top, (ty + 1) * TILE];
23	}
24	
25	/** 单个轴的移动 + 碰撞解析。返回是否发生碰撞。 */
26	function moveAxis(b: Body, world: World, dx: number, dy: number): { hitX: boolean; hitY: boolean } {
27	  let hitX = false, hitY = false;
28	  const st = world.store;
29	  const avx = Math.abs(b.vx);
30	  // 原版坡面放行门（Collision.cs:2361-2387 flag3）：从高/低侧贴面走近的坡面格
31	  // 不参与本轴碰撞（交给 slopeCollide 对角线贴合）。全部以【移动前】位置判定
32	  // （原版 vector3,:2306）——传参 ox/oy,勿用移动后的 b.x/b.y。
33	  // 地面坡门是 feet-|vx| <= 格底(top+16,:2375/:2379 的 num7=格高)——不是格顶!
34	  // 写成格顶会让低侧贴地进入永远不过 → 孤立坡前一格被 X 拦截卡死。
35	  // slope1 左高右低 / 2 右高左低 / 3 左低右高(天花板) / 4 右低左高(天花板)
36	  const slopePass = (tx: number, ty: number, ox: number, oy: number): boolean => {
37	    const j = st.idx(tx, ty);
38	    const sl = st.slope[j];
39	    if (sl === 0 || st.half[j]) return false;
40	    const top = ty * TILE;
41	    if (sl === 1) return oy + b.h - avx <= top + TILE && ox >= tx * TILE;
42	    if (sl === 2) return oy + b.h - avx <= top + TILE && ox + b.w <= tx * TILE + TILE;
43	    if (sl === 3) return oy + avx >= top && ox >= tx * TILE;
44	    return oy + avx >= top && ox + b.w <= tx * TILE + TILE; // 4
45	  };
46	  // X 轴
47	  if (dx !== 0) {
48	    b.x += dx;
49	    const dir = Math.sign(dx);
50	    const oldX = b.x - dx; // 本步移动前位置（原版 vector3）
51	    const edgeX = dir > 0 ? b.x + b.w : b.x;
52	    const tx = Math.floor(edgeX / TILE);
53	    const y0 = Math.floor(b.y / TILE), y1 = Math.floor((b.y + b.h - 0.01) / TILE);
54	    for (let ty = y0; ty <= y1; ty++) {
55	      if (slopePass(tx, ty, oldX, b.y)) continue;
56	      const span = solidSpan(world, tx, ty);
57	      // 半砖只占下半：身体底部没超过半砖顶面（ty*16+8）则不拦
58	      if (span && b.y + b.h > span[0]) {
59	        // 原版拦截前提（:2406/:2426）：上一位置【完全】在该格一侧（贴面接近）才拦；
60	        // 身体已横向跨在格上（上坡爬升中段,脚沉在斜面下）不拦——交给 slopeCollide
61	        // 抬升,否则上坡会被每帧推回卡死
62	        if (dir > 0 ? oldX + b.w > tx * TILE + 0.01 : oldX < (tx + 1) * TILE - 0.01) continue;
63	        // hoik 坡链放行（:2412/:2432）：身后格是配套坡面（同向坡链中段）→ 不拦
64	        const bj = st.idx(tx - Math.sign(dir), ty);
65	        if (dir > 0 && (st.slope[bj] === 2 || st.slope[bj] === 4)) continue;
66	        if (dir < 0 && (st.slope[bj] === 1 || st.slope[bj] === 3)) continue;
67	        if (dir > 0) b.x = tx * TILE - b.w;
68	        else b.x = (tx + 1) * TILE;
69	        b.vx = 0;
70	        hitX = true;
71	        break;
72	      }
73	    }
74	  }
75	  // Y 轴
76	  if (dy !== 0) {
77	    const oldY = b.y; // 移动前位置（原版 vector3.Y）
78	    b.y += dy;
79	    const dir = Math.sign(dy);
80	    const edgeY = dir > 0 ? b.y + b.h : b.y;
81	    const ty = Math.floor(edgeY / TILE);
82	    const x0 = Math.floor(b.x / TILE), x1 = Math.floor((b.x + b.w - 0.01) / TILE);
83	    // 原版取整行最高面（Collision.cs:1610-1631 num13 取最大盒顶），不能 break 在最左列——
84	    // 否则左列半砖/右列整砖时身体会嵌进整砖 8px
85	    let bestTop = Infinity;
86	    for (let tx = x0; tx <= x1; tx++) {
87	      const solid = world.store.isSolid(tx, ty);
88	      const span = solidSpan(world, tx, ty);
89	      const plat = !b.dropThrough && dir > 0 && world.store.isPlatform(tx, ty)
90	        && (b.y + b.h) - dy <= ty * TILE + 1; // 上一位置在平台之上
91	      if (dir > 0) {
92	        if (!solid && !plat) continue;
93	        if (slopePass(tx, ty, b.x, oldY)) continue; // 坡面格交给 slopeCollide 对角线贴合
94	        // 落地门槛（Collision.cs:1610/1631）：新底部越过盒顶 且 上一位置在盒顶之上，
95	        // 否则（嵌入/侧入）不吸附——半砖盒顶是 ty*16+8，只进入行上半不算落地
96	        const top = span ? span[0] : ty * TILE;
97	        if (b.y + b.h <= top || (b.y + b.h) - dy > top + 0.01) continue;
98	        if (top < bestTop) bestTop = top;
99	      } else {
100	        if (!solid) continue;
101	        if (slopePass(tx, ty, b.x, oldY)) continue;
102	        // 上顶：上一位置在盒底之下才命中；取最低盒底（最先撞到的天花板）
103	        const bottom = (ty + 1) * TILE;
104	        if (b.y >= bottom || b.y - dy < bottom - 0.01) continue;
105	        if (bottom < bestTop) bestTop = bottom;
106	      }
107	    }
108	    if (bestTop !== Infinity) {
109	      if (dir > 0) { b.y = bestTop - b.h; b.onGround = true; }
110	      else { b.y = bestTop; b.hitHead = true; }
111	      b.vy = 0;
112	      hitY = true;
113	    }
114	  }
115	  return { hitX, hitY };
116	}
117	
118	/** 原版 Collision.SlopeCollision（1456 Collision.cs:1796-2036）适配：
119	 *  常规碰撞后对重叠坡面格做对角线贴合——身体沉到斜面下时抬回斜面上
120	 *  （走路沿坡爬升;多格取最高贴合位,与原版 y 最小值机制一致）。
121	 *  slope1/2 地面坡:slope1 左高右低(左行爬升)、slope2 右高左低;
122	 *  slope3/4 天花板坡:对称向下推离。
123	 *  受阻回退已对齐原版 :2004-2033（速度清零 + slope 向性 X 补偿）。
124	 *  与原版的偏差（稳定性取舍,均在行内注释标记）：
125	 *  - num4 钳 ≥0（原版 num4<0 跳过）：坡顶过渡瞬间防脱钩下沉
126	 *  - 未移植 :2003 的"重跑 TileCollision 校验"本体（分轴结构代价高,
127	 *    钳位已覆盖其主收益;受阻回退语义由嵌入守卫分支等价提供） */
128	function slopeCollide(b: Body, world: World): void {
129	  const st = world.store;
130	  const x0 = Math.floor(b.x / TILE), x1 = Math.floor((b.x + b.w - 0.01) / TILE);
131	  const y0 = Math.floor(b.y / TILE), y1 = Math.floor((b.y + b.h - 0.01) / TILE);
132	  let bestLift = 0;      // 最大抬升量（负 y 位移;0 = 无贴合）
133	  let bestDrop = 0;      // 天花板最大下推量
134	  for (let tx = x0; tx <= x1; tx++) {
135	    for (let ty = y0; ty <= y1; ty++) {
136	      if (!st.inBounds(tx, ty)) continue;
137	      const i = st.idx(tx, ty);
138	      const slope = st.slope[i];
139	      if (slope === 0 || st.half[i] || !st.isSolid(tx, ty)) continue;
140	      const vx0 = tx * TILE, vy0 = ty * TILE;
141	      if (b.x + b.w <= vx0 || b.x >= vx0 + TILE || b.y + b.h <= vy0 || b.y >= vy0 + TILE) continue;
142	      // 对角线水平偏移（:1882-1893/1927-1934）：slope1/3 取身体左缘进格深度,
143	      // slope2/4 取右缘。钳到 ≥0（与原版 num4<0 即跳过的差异,见函数头注释）：
144	      // 坡顶过渡瞬间(高侧缘越过坡面)原版会脱钩,脚底残留在对角线端点下方数像素,
145	      // 落地门槛不补救 → 角色沉进坡格;钳位后贴合保持到水平重叠结束,送脚到
146	      // 对角线高端点(=相邻整砖顶),由常规落地无缝接管
147	      let num4: number;
148	      if (slope === 1 || slope === 3) num4 = Math.max(0, b.x - vx0);
149	      else num4 = Math.max(0, vx0 + TILE - (b.x + b.w));
150	      if (slope === 3 || slope === 4) {
151	        // 天花板坡（:1883-1921）：头在斜面上方才贴合下推
152	        if (b.y <= vy0 + TILE - num4) {
153	          const num5 = vy0 + TILE - b.y - num4;
154	          if (num5 > bestDrop) bestDrop = num5;
155	        }
156	      } else {
157	        // 地面坡（:1935-1966）：脚沉到斜面之下才抬回（num7<0 即抬升）
158	        if (b.y + b.h < vy0 + num4) continue;
159	        const num7 = vy0 - (b.y + b.h) + num4;
160	        if (num7 < bestLift) bestLift = num7;
161	      }
162	    }
163	  }
164	  if (bestLift < 0) {
165	    const ny = b.y + bestLift;
166	    // 抬升后不能嵌进实心
167	    let blocked = false;
168	    const bx0 = Math.floor(b.x / TILE), bx1 = Math.floor((b.x + b.w - 0.01) / TILE);
169	    const by0 = Math.floor(ny / TILE), by1 = Math.floor((ny + b.h - 0.01) / TILE);
170	    for (let tx = bx0; tx <= bx1 && !blocked; tx++) {
171	      for (let ty = by0; ty <= by1; ty++) {
172	        if (!st.inBounds(tx, ty)) continue;
173	        const j = st.idx(tx, ty);
174	        if (!st.isSolid(tx, ty) || st.slope[j] > 0 || st.half[j]) continue;
175	        const top = ty * TILE + (st.half[j] ? 8 : 0);
176	        if (b.x + b.w > tx * TILE && b.x < tx * TILE + TILE && ny + b.h > top && ny < (ty + 1) * TILE) { blocked = true; break; }
177	      }
178	    }
179	    if (!blocked) {
180	      b.y = ny;
181	      if (b.vy > 0) b.vy = 0;
182	      b.onGround = true;
183	    } else {
184	      // 抬升受阻回退（原版 :2004-2018 的速度清零语义）——墙角坡干净挡停而非楔进角落。
185	      // 原版另有按 shortfall 的 X 滑动补偿,但其"重跑校验"保证每帧只作用一次;
186	      // 我们的分轴结构下输入每帧重新加速,X 推会变棘轮,故只取清零、不推 X
187	      b.vx = 0;
188	      b.vy = 0;
189	    }
190	  }
191	  if (bestDrop > 0) {
192	    // 下推守卫:推离后不能嵌进下方实心(对称于抬升守卫)
193	    const ny = b.y + bestDrop;
194	    let blocked = false;
195	    const bx0 = Math.floor(b.x / TILE), bx1 = Math.floor((b.x + b.w - 0.01) / TILE);
196	    const by0 = Math.floor(ny / TILE), by1 = Math.floor((ny + b.h - 0.01) / TILE);
197	    for (let tx = bx0; tx <= bx1 && !blocked; tx++) {
198	      for (let ty = by0; ty <= by1; ty++) {
199	        if (!st.inBounds(tx, ty)) continue;
200	        const j = st.idx(tx, ty);
201	        if (!st.isSolid(tx, ty) || st.slope[j] > 0 || st.half[j]) continue;
202	        const top = ty * TILE + (st.half[j] ? 8 : 0);
203	        if (b.x + b.w > tx * TILE && b.x < tx * TILE + TILE && ny + b.h > top && ny < (ty + 1) * TILE) { blocked = true; break; }
204	      }
205	    }
206	    if (!blocked) {
207	      b.y = ny;
208	      if (b.vy < 0.0101) b.vy = 0.0101; // 原版向下推离（:1902-1905）
209	    } else {
210	      // 天花板坡下推受阻（原版 :2020-2033 镜像）：速度清零（X 补偿同上不取）
211	      b.vx = 0;
212	      b.vy = 0;
213	    }
214	  }
215	}
216	
217	/** 移动并碰撞。高速时分子步防穿透。 */
218	export function moveAndCollide(b: Body, world: World, dx: number, dy: number) {
219	  b.onGround = false;
220	  b.hitWall = false;
221	  b.hitHead = false;
222	  // 速度辅助（Collision.cs:1773-1790）：脚底中心所在格(原版为整行落地候选格的近似)
223	  // 是 slope1/2、水平速度朝低侧、脚已沉到对角线 → dy += |dx|
224	  // （等价 Velocity.Y += |Velocity.X|）把身体粘到斜面,消除坡顶角/高速下坡 1 帧的格顶滑行
225	  {
226	    const st = world.store;
227	    const feetRow = Math.floor((b.y + b.h) / TILE);
228	    const midCol = Math.floor((b.x + b.w / 2) / TILE);
229	    if (dx !== 0 && st.inBounds(midCol, feetRow)) {
230	      const fi = st.idx(midCol, feetRow);
231	      const fs = st.slope[fi];
232	      if ((fs === 1 || fs === 2) && !st.half[fi] && st.isSolid(midCol, feetRow)) {
233	        const vx0 = midCol * TILE;
234	        const num4 = fs === 1 ? b.x - vx0 : vx0 + TILE - (b.x + b.w);
235	        if (num4 >= 0 && b.y + b.h >= feetRow * TILE + num4
236	          && ((fs === 1 && b.vx > 0) || (fs === 2 && b.vx < 0))) {
237	          dy += Math.abs(dx);
238	        }
239	      }
240	    }
241	  }
242	  const maxStep = 8;
243	  const steps = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(Math.max(Math.abs(dx), Math.abs(dy)) / maxStep));
244	  for (let i = 0; i < steps; i++) {
245	    const sx = dx / steps, sy = dy / steps;
246	    const { hitX, hitY } = moveAxis(b, world, sx, sy);
247	    if (hitX) { b.hitWall = true; dx = 0; }
248	    if (hitY) dy = 0;
249	    if (dx === 0 && dy === 0) break;
250	  }
251	  slopeCollide(b, world);
252	}
253	

```

    player.fixedUpdate(dt, this);
    // ---- 起爆器 411 坠落触发（Player.cs:34368 TryLandingOnDetonator）：落地冲击 vy≥3 且
    // 脚下格（Bottom 左下角 +0.01）是 411 未触发态（frameY==0 && frameX<36）→ Wiring.HitSwitch
    if (player.landImpactVy >= 3 && this.wiring) {
      const dx = Math.floor(player.x / TILE), dy = Math.floor((player.y + player.h + 0.01) / TILE);
      const di = this.world.store.idx(dx, dy);
      if (this.wiring.sheetOf(dx, dy) === 411
        && this.world.store.frameY[di] === 0 && this.world.store.frameX[di] < 36) {
        this.wiring.hitSwitch(dx, dy);
      }
      player.landImpactVy = 0; // 消费（防多帧重复）
    }
    this.updateTallGateAuto();
    if (player.dead) {
      // 死亡瞬间：红色粉碎粒子 + 原地留墓碑（只处理一次）
      if (!this._deathHandled) {
        this._deathHandled = true;
        this.handlePlayerDeath();
      }
      // 重生计时
      player.respawnTimer++;
      if (player.respawnTimer > 180) this.respawnPlayer();
    } else {
      this._deathHandled = false;
    }

    // ---- 智能光标（每帧 lookup；结果供 updateUse 覆盖目标格 + 渲染黄框） ----
    this.updateSmartCursor();
    // ---- 使用物品（鼠标） ----
    this.updateUse(dt);
    this.prevSwingMouse = !!this.input?.mouseDown; // 挥剑点击沿（非 autoReuse 武器需重新点击）

    // ---- 实体 ----
    this.entities.update(dt, this);

    // ---- 液体（原版每 2 tick 一次 UpdateLiquid）----
    if (this.tickCount % 2 === 0) {
      this.liquid.step();
      // 液体直接写数组绕过 store 监听：有活动液体时手动通知光照衰减变化
      if (this.liquid.numLiquid > 0) this.lighting.liquidDirty = true;

    this.heldWaterCandle = heldWC;

// 掉落物实体：重力、落地、磁吸拾取
import { Entity } from './Entity';
import type { GameHooks } from './types';
import { GRAVITY, MAX_FALL_SPEED, MAGNET_RADIUS, PICKUP_DELAY, TILE } from '../core/constants';
import { moveAndCollide } from '../physics/TileCollision';
import { ITEM_DEFS } from '../data/items';
import type { Player } from './Player';

export class ItemDrop extends Entity {
  w = 12; h = 12;
  itemId: number;
  stack: number;
  age = 0;
  bobPhase: number;
  /** 原版 Item.color（凝胶等掉落继承怪物色，逐像素乘法贴轮廓渲染） */
  color?: number[]; // [r, g, b, a]
  /** 心(58)/星(184) 是 pickup 类物品（ItemID.Sets.IsAPickup, ItemID.cs:248）：
   *  碰触即回血 20 / 回蓝 100 并消失，永不进背包（Player.PickupItem :34610-34630） */
  pickup?: 'heart' | 'star';

  constructor(x: number, y: number, itemId: number, stack = 1, vx = 0, vy = -2) {
    super();
    this.x = x; this.y = y;
    this.itemId = itemId;
    this.stack = stack;
    this.vx = vx;
    this.vy = vy;
    this.bobPhase = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;
  }

  fixedUpdate(dt: number, game: GameHooks) {
    this.age++;
    // 寿命（10 分钟）
    if (this.age > 60 * 600) { this.dead = true; return; }

    const player = (game as unknown as { player: Player }).player;
    let beingGrabbed = false;
    if (player && !player.dead && this.age > PICKUP_DELAY) {
      // 原版 GrabItems(Player.cs:34461-34524):hitbox 相交=直接拾取;
      // 否则玩家盒 ±42px(defaultItemGrabRange :2406)扩展盒相交=拉取；
      // 宝藏磁石(+94)/金戒指族(+12)扩展范围（Player.grabRangeBonus 聚合）
      const GRAB_RANGE = 42 + ((player as { grabRangeBonus?: number }).grabRangeBonus ?? 0)
        // 拾心药水（lifeMagnet）：红心单独扩大拾取范围
        + (this.pickup === 'heart' ? ((player as { heartGrabBonus?: number }).heartGrabBonus ?? 0) : 0)
        // 魔力磁石（manaMagnet：星 184/1735/1868 专用吸附，Player.cs:34495 专属拉取通道）
        + (this.pickup === 'star' ? ((player as { manaMagnetBonus?: number }).manaMagnetBonus ?? 0) : 0);
      const touching =
        this.x < player.x + player.w && this.x + this.w > player.x &&
        this.y < player.y + player.h && this.y + this.h > player.y;
      const inGrabRange =
        this.x < player.x + player.w + GRAB_RANGE && this.x + this.w > player.x - GRAB_RANGE &&
        this.y < player.y + player.h + GRAB_RANGE && this.y + this.h > player.y - GRAB_RANGE;
      if (touching) {
        // 心/星 pickup：直接回复并消失，不进背包（原版拾取即 Heal(20)/mana+100+ClearOut）
        if (this.pickup) {
          if (this.pickup === 'heart') {
            player.hp = Math.min(player.maxHp, player.hp + 20);
            game.addDamageNumber(player.cx, player.y, 20, false, '#40E080');
          } else {
            player.mana = Math.min(player.maxMana, player.mana + 100);
            game.addDamageNumber(player.cx, player.y, 100, false, '#4080FF');
          }
          this.dead = true;
          game.playSfx('pickup', 0.75); // 原版 SoundID 7 Grab
          return;
        }
        const before = this.stack;
        const left = player.inv.add(this.itemId, this.stack);
        if (left === 0) {
          this.dead = true;
          game.notifyInventoryChanged();
          // 铜币拾取用专属音效，其余走通用拾取
          // 音量略低（0.75）：拾取与挖掘声同时触发时两者都可闻，不被 Grab 盖住
          game.playSfx(ITEM_DEFS[this.itemId]?.key === 'coin_copper' ? 'coin' : 'pickup', 0.75);
          const def = ITEM_DEFS[this.itemId];
          if (def) game.showPickupLabel(def.key);
          return;
        }
        if (left !== before) game.notifyInventoryChanged();
        this.stack = left;
      } else if (inGrabRange) {
        // PullItem_Common(:34533-34584):每轴 0.45 步进、钳 4(水平含 player.vx),
        // 速度反向时附加 ×0.75 反拉——被拉取帧跳过瓦片碰撞(WorldItem.cs:587-597
        // else 分支仅 position+=velocity),物品穿墙飞向玩家
        beingGrabbed = true;
        const SPEED = 0.45, MAXV = 4, BACK = 0.75;
        if (player.cx > this.cx) {
          if (this.vx < MAXV + player.vx) this.vx += SPEED;
          if (this.vx < 0) this.vx += SPEED * BACK;
        } else {
          if (this.vx > -MAXV + player.vx) this.vx -= SPEED;
          if (this.vx > 0) this.vx -= SPEED * BACK;
        }
        if (player.cy > this.cy) {
          if (this.vy < MAXV) this.vy += SPEED;
          if (this.vy < 0) this.vy += SPEED * BACK;
        } else {
          if (this.vy > -MAXV) this.vy -= SPEED;
          if (this.vy > 0) this.vy -= SPEED * BACK;
        }
      }
    }

    if (beingGrabbed) {
      // 原版被抓取帧:无重力无碰撞,直接位移(WorldItem.cs:596 ApplyMovement)
      this.x += this.vx;
      this.y += this.vy;
    } else {
      this.vy = Math.min(this.vy + GRAVITY * 0.7, MAX_FALL_SPEED);
      this.vx *= 0.96;
      moveAndCollide(this, game.world, this.vx, this.vy);
    }
    // 入水小水花声（掉落物轻，音量小）
    const st = game.world.store;
    const li = st.liquid[st.idx(Math.floor(this.cx / TILE), Math.floor(this.cy / TILE))];
    const wasWater = this.inWater;
    this.inWater = li > 80;
    if (this.inWater && !wasWater && this.vy > 1.2) game.playSfx('splash', 0.35);
    void ITEM_DEFS;
  }

```
  /** 原版 inActive()：致动器翻转后的"缩入地下"态（有 type 但不碰撞不阻挡） */
  isActuated(x: number, y: number): boolean {
    return this.inBounds(x, y) && (this.wire[this.idx(x, y)] & TileStore.WIRE_ACTUATED) !== 0;
  }

  isSolid(x: number, y: number): boolean {
    if (!this.inBounds(x, y)) return false; // 世界外视为空（边缘自由）
    if (this.wire[this.idx(x, y)] & TileStore.WIRE_ACTUATED) return false; // 已致动不碰撞
    const d = TILE_DEFS[this.type[this.idx(x, y)]];
    return !!d && d.solid;
  }

  isPlatform(x: number, y: number): boolean {
    if (!this.inBounds(x, y)) return false;
    const d = TILE_DEFS[this.type[this.idx(x, y)]];
    return !!d && d.platform;
  }

  onTileChanged(fn: (x: number, y: number) => void) {
    this.listeners.push(fn);
  }

// 内部 tile / wall 注册表。id 即数组下标。
// 数值（硬度/光照）为原创平衡设计；mapColor 参考 TEdit tiles.json 的地图配色后重新取整。

export interface TileDef {
  key: string;
  name: string;
  solid: boolean;        // 参与碰撞
  platform: boolean;     // 单向平台（下跳可穿）
  decor: boolean;        // 装饰物（不碰撞、可被任何工具秒清）
  framed: boolean;       // 多格框架物体（使用 frameX/frameY 定位贴图）
  opacity: number;       // 光照阻挡 0-1
  light?: [number, number, number]; // 自发光 RGB
  pick: number;          // 所需镐力（-1 不可挖）
  axe: number;           // 所需斧力
  mapColor: string;      // 小地图颜色
  drop?: string | null;   // 破坏掉落 item key（缺省掉同名 key；null = 显式无掉落,原版装饰类）
  rope?: boolean;        // 绳索类（攀爬判定）
  blend: boolean;        // 是否参与同类边缘融合
  attach?: 'ground' | 'wall'; // 装饰物附着需求
  w?: number; h?: number;     // framed 物体占格数
  vanilla?: {                 // 原版素材渲染（terraria-assets + TEdit 数据）
    sheet: number;            // Tiles_N 表 id
    frame: 'auto' | 'style' | 'blend'; // auto=47 表 8 向；style=显式帧；blend=BlendRules 合并分帧（泥土/石/草族）
    fw?: number; fh?: number; // style 类的占格数（TEdit frameSize）
  };
}

export const T = {
  EMPTY: 0, DIRT: 1, STONE: 2, GRASS: 3,
  ORE_COPPER: 4, ORE_IRON: 5, ORE_SILVER: 6, ORE_GOLD: 7,
  TREE: 8, LEAVES: 9, WOOD: 10, PLATFORM: 11,
  TORCH: 12, WORKBENCH: 13, FURNACE: 14, ANVIL: 15,
  CHEST: 16, DOOR_CLOSED: 17, DOOR_OPEN: 18,
  MUSHROOM: 19, FLOWER: 20, TALLGRASS: 21, SAND: 22, SNOW: 23,
  SAPLING: 24, ASH: 25,
} as const;

export const TILE_DEFS: TileDef[] = [];
function def(d: Partial<TileDef> & { key: string }): number {
  const id = TILE_DEFS.length;
  TILE_DEFS.push({
    name: d.key, solid: false, platform: false, decor: false, framed: false,
    opacity: 0, pick: -1, axe: -1, mapColor: '#000', blend: false, ...d, id,
  } as TileDef);
  return id;
}
// 保证 id 与 T 常量一致（按顺序注册）
def({ key: 'empty', name: '空气' });
def({ key: 'dirt', name: '泥土', solid: true, opacity: 1, pick: 0, mapColor: '#976B4B', blend: true, drop: 'dirt_block', vanilla: { sheet: 0, frame: 'blend' } });
def({ key: 'stone', name: '石块', solid: true, opacity: 1, pick: 0, mapColor: '#808080', blend: true, drop: 'stone_block', vanilla: { sheet: 1, frame: 'blend' } });
def({ key: 'grass', name: '草块', solid: true, opacity: 1, pick: 0, mapColor: '#1CD85E', blend: true, drop: 'dirt_block', vanilla: { sheet: 2, frame: 'blend' } });
def({ key: 'ore_copper', name: '铜矿', solid: true, opacity: 1, pick: 0, mapColor: '#964316', blend: false, drop: 'copper_ore', vanilla: { sheet: 7, frame: 'auto' } });
def({ key: 'ore_iron', name: '铁矿', solid: true, opacity: 1, pick: 0, mapColor: '#8C6A56', blend: false, drop: 'iron_ore', vanilla: { sheet: 6, frame: 'auto' } });
def({ key: 'ore_silver', name: '银矿', solid: true, opacity: 1, pick: 20, mapColor: '#B9C2C3', blend: false, drop: 'silver_ore', vanilla: { sheet: 9, frame: 'auto' } });

def({ key: 'leaves', name: '树叶', decor: true, framed: true, opacity: 0.05, pick: 0, axe: 0, mapColor: '#2E8B3D', drop: null });

93:  private sheetAt(x: number, y: number): number { return this.sheetOf(x, y); }
95:  sheetOf(x: number, y: number): number {
  private sheetAt(x: number, y: number): number { return this.sheetOf(x, y); }
  /** 该格的原版 tile id(sheet;非 vanilla tile 返回 -1)。器件表按 sheet 分发 */
  sheetOf(x: number, y: number): number {
    if (!this.st.inBounds(x, y)) return -1;
    const d = TILE_DEFS[this.st.type[this.p2i(x, y)]];
    return d?.vanilla?.sheet ?? -1;
  }
  /** 多格器件整体去重(devices.ts 用) */
  skipCell(x: number, y: number): void { this.skipWire(x, y); }

import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import { World } from '../src/world/World';
import { Player } from '../src/entities/Player';
import { Inventory } from '../src/items/Inventory';
import { TILE_BY_KEY } from '../src/data/tiles';

// 黏滞 tile（Collision.StickyTiles + Player.cs:22650-22740）
const fakeHooks = (w: World) => ({
  world: w,
  spawnDrop: () => null,
  addDamageNumber: () => {},
  playSfx: () => {},
  spawnParticles: () => {},
  onInventoryChanged: () => {},
});

describe('蛛网/蜂蜜块黏滞（StickyTiles 1:1）', () => {
  it('蛛网内：下落钳 1 且 ×0.3 缓沉、水平 ×0.6/0.85 阻尼、禁跳', () => {
    const w = new World(40, 40, 42, 'web');
    const st = w.store;
    const WEB = TILE_BY_KEY['v_51_cobweb']!;
    // 20,20 放一列 4 格蛛网
    for (let y = 18; y <= 21; y++) st.setTileSilent(20, y, WEB);
    const p = new Player(20 * 16, 17 * 16, new Inventory());
    p.vy = 8; p.vx = 3; p.jumpHold = 10; // 高速下落 + 横移 + 跳跃中
    p.fixedUpdate(1 / 60, fakeHooks(w) as never);
    expect(p.vy).toBeLessThanOrEqual(1 * 0.3 + 0.36); // 钳 1 后 ×0.3 + 本 tick 重力
    expect(p.jumpHold).toBe(0);
    expect(p.vx).toBeLessThan(1.5); // 水平阻尼明显
  });

  it('无黏滞 tile 时不受影响', () => {
    const w = new World(40, 40, 42, 'open');
    const p = new Player(20 * 16, 17 * 16, new Inventory());
    p.vy = 8;
    p.fixedUpdate(1 / 60, fakeHooks(w) as never);
    expect(p.vy).toBeGreaterThan(4); // 正常重力累积
  });

  it('蜂蜜块：Y 缓沉生效但不禁跳', () => {
    const w = new World(40, 40, 42, 'honey');
    const st = w.store;
    const HONEY = TILE_BY_KEY['v_229_honey_block']!;
    for (let y = 18; y <= 21; y++) st.setTileSilent(20, y, HONEY);
    const p = new Player(20 * 16, 17 * 16, new Inventory());
    p.vy = 8; p.jumpHold = 10;
    p.inputJump = true; // 按住跳：正常每 tick -1（=9）；若被黏滞清零会是 0

// 传送带实体输送 1:1

//  - TileID.Sets.ConveyorDirection（TileID.cs:261）：{421:+1, 422:-1}
import { TILE } from '../core/constants';
import { TILE_DEFS } from '../data/tiles';
import type { TileStore } from '../world/TileStore';

export interface ConveyorBody { x: number; y: number; w: number; h: number; vx: number; vy?: number }

const EPS = 0.01;

/** 原版 TileID.Sets.ConveyorDirection（TileID.cs:261）按 vanilla sheet 查表：
 *  421(Conveyor Belt Clockwise,素材键 ...conveyorbeltleft) → +1；422(CCW) → -1；其余 0。
 *  注意：方向符号 = 站上带后的水平推运方向（Collision.cs:4333 velocity.X = num*gravDir，
 *  num = Σ ConveyorDirection×num5×gravDir，站立 num5=+1）→ 421 向 +X（右）、422 向 -X（左）。
 *  "ConveyorBeltLeft/Right" 的素材命名与推运方向无关（物品名是 Clockwise/CCW，
 *  idNames.generated.ts:11361-11362）。 */
export function conveyorSheetOf(type: number): number { return TILE_DEFS[type]?.vanilla?.sheet ?? -1; }

export function conveyorDirection(type: number): number {
  const s = TILE_DEFS[type]?.vanilla?.sheet;
  if (s === 421) return 1;
  if (s === 422) return -1;
  return 0;
}

```ts
function clampMove(ent, st, dx, dy): void {
  if (dx !== 0) {
    const dir = Math.sign(dx);
    const oldX = ent.x;
    ent.x += dx;
    const edge = dir > 0 ? ent.x + ent.w : ent.x;
    const tx = Math.floor(edge / TILE);
    const y0 = Math.floor(ent.y / TILE), y1 = Math.floor((ent.y + ent.h - 0.01) / TILE);
    for (let ty = y0; ty <= y1; ty++) {
      const j = st.idx(tx, ty); // careful bounds
      if (!st.inBounds(tx, ty)) continue;
      if (!st.isSolid(tx, ty)) continue;
      const top = ty * TILE + (st.half[j] ? 8 : 0);
      if (ent.y + ent.h > top) { ent.x = dir > 0 ? tx * TILE - ent.w : (tx + 1) * TILE; break; }
    }
    void oldX;
  }
  if (dy !== 0) { ... similar downward glue: only clamp when dy>0 onto solid/platform top ... }
}
```

Another vanilla subtlety: velocity normalize — if num2 != 0 the step is diagonal 2.5 magnitude (so horizontal component < 2.5 on slopes). Implement with Math.hypot.

```
  constructor(w: number, h: number, seed: number, name = '新世界') {
    this.store = new TileStore(w, h);
    this.explored = new Uint8Array(w * h);
    this.seed = seed;
    this.name = name;
    // 1 游戏日 = 30 现实分钟（24→40 后折中）
    this.clock = new Clock(30 * 60 * 1000);
  }

  /** 从 worker 数据包重建（buffer 已 transfer 移交，零拷贝包装）。
   *  explored 未包含在包内（saveGame 不持久化、新生成/读档均全零）时按全零分配 */
  static fromPacket(p: import('../workers/protocol').WorldPacket): World {
    const w = new World(p.w, p.h, p.seed, p.name);
    w.store = new TileStore(p.w, p.h, p.buf);
    w.explored = p.buf.explored ? new Uint8Array(p.buf.explored) : new Uint8Array(p.w * p.h);
    w.spawnX = p.spawnX; w.spawnY = p.spawnY;
    w.groundLevel = p.groundLevel; w.rockLevel = p.rockLevel; w.lavaLine = p.lavaLine;
    w.crimson = p.crimson; w.dungeonX = p.dungeonX; w.dungeonY = p.dungeonY; w.jungleX = p.jungleX;
    w.exploredVersion = p.exploredVersion;
    w.clock.timeOfDay = p.clock.timeOfDay;
    w.clock.dayCount = p.clock.dayCount;
    w.chests = p.chests;
    w.signs = p.signs;
    w.trees = p.trees;
    w.flags = p.flags;
    w.treeX = p.treeX; w.treeStyle = p.treeStyle; w.treeTops = p.treeTops;
    w.shimmerX = p.shimmerX ?? 0; w.shimmerY = p.shimmerY ?? 0;
    return w;
  }

  get w() { return this.store.w; }
  get h() { return this.store.h; }
  get tiles() { return this.store; }

  /** 破坏一个 tile（含 framed 物体的锚点处理由调用方负责），返回掉落 item key */
  breakTileAt(x: number, y: number): string | null {
    const t = this.store.get(x, y);
    if (t === 0) return null;
    const d = TILE_DEFS[t];
    this.store.setTile(x, y, 0);
    // v_ 图块无显式 drop → 回退同名放置物品(阶段5 自动注册)。
    // drop:null = 显式无掉落(原版装饰类)——?? 会把 null 也走兜底,必须显式判 undefined
    return d.drop === undefined ? (d.key.startsWith('v_') ? 'place_' + d.key : null) : d.drop;
  }

  isSolidPx(px: number, py: number): boolean {
    return this.store.isSolid(Math.floor(px / 16), Math.floor(py / 16));
  }

  /** 液体量（0-255）按像素坐标 */
  liquidAt(px: number, py: number): number {
    return this.store.liquid[this.store.idx(Math.floor(px / 16), Math.floor(py / 16))];
  }

  registerTree(x: number, y: number, h: number) {
    this.trees.push({ x, y, h });
  }
}

```ts
function clampMove(ent, st, dx, dy) {
  if (dx !== 0) {
    const dir = dx > 0 ? 1 : -1;
    const oldRight = ent.x + ent.w, oldLeft = ent.x;
    ent.x += dx;
    const edge = dir > 0 ? ent.x + ent.w : ent.x;
    const tx = Math.floor(edge / TILE);
    const y0 = Math.floor(ent.y / TILE), y1 = Math.floor((ent.y + ent.h - 0.01) / TILE);
    for (let ty = y0; ty <= y1; ty++) {
      if (!st.inBounds(tx, ty)) continue;
      const j = st.idx(tx, ty);
      if (!st.isSolid(tx, ty) || st.slope[j] > 0) continue;   // 坡面格交给斜面贴合(与 moveAxis 一致)
      const top = ty * TILE + (st.half[j] ? 8 : 0);
      if (ent.y + ent.h <= top) continue;
      // 贴面接近才拦（moveAxis :62 同款守卫）：已横跨该格不拦
      if (dir > 0 ? oldRight > tx * TILE + 0.01 : oldLeft < (tx + 1) * TILE - 0.01) continue;
      ent.x = dir > 0 ? tx * TILE - ent.w : (tx + 1) * TILE;
      break;
    }
  }
  if (dy !== 0) {
    const dir = dy > 0 ? 1 : -1;
    const prevBottom = ent.y + ent.h, prevTop = ent.y;
    ent.y += dy;
    const edgeY = dir > 0 ? ent.y + ent.h : ent.y;
    const ty = Math.floor(edgeY / TILE);
    const x0 = Math.floor(ent.x / TILE), x1 = Math.floor((ent.x + ent.w - 0.01) / TILE);
    let best = Infinity;
    for (let tx = x0; tx <= x1; tx++) {
      if (!st.inBounds(tx, ty)) continue;
      const j = st.idx(tx, ty);
      const solid = st.isSolid(tx, ty) && st.slope[j] === 0;
      const span = solid ? ty * TILE + (st.half[j] ? 8 : 0) : -1;
      const plat = dir > 0 && !ent.dropThrough && st.isPlatform(tx, ty) && prevBottom <= ty * TILE + 1;
      if (dir > 0) {
        const top = span >= 0 ? span : (plat ? ty * TILE : -1);
        if (top < 0) continue;
        if (ent.y + ent.h <= top || prevBottom > top + 0.01) continue;
        if (top < best) best = top;
      } else { ... ceiling ... }
    }
    if (best !== Infinity) { ent.y = dir > 0 ? best - ent.h : best; }
  }
}
```

接触容差（见文件头偏差说明） */\nconst EPS = 0.1;\n\n/** 被输送实体最小形状（Entity/ItemDrop 均满足） */\nexport interface ConveyorBody {\n  x: number; y: number; w: number; h: number;\n  vx: number;\n  /** 平台单向碰撞下落开关（glue 下压时判平台用；掉落物无此字段） */\n  dropThrough?: boolean;\n}\n\n/** 原版 TileID.Sets.ConveyorDirection（TileID.cs:261）：421→+1、422→-1、其余 0。\n *  以 vanilla sheet 查表（同 Wiring.sheetOf 口径），与内部 tile id 解耦。 */\nexport function conveyorDirection(tileType: number): number {\n  const sheet = TILE_DEFS[tileType]?.vanilla?.sheet;\n  return sheet === 421 ? 1 : sheet === 422 ? -1 : 0;\n}\n\n/** NPCID.Sets.ConveyorBeltCollision（NPCID.cs:4789，CreateBoolSet 全员=true）：\n *  这些敌怪例外地会被传送带搬运（其余普通敌怪原版不吃带）。 */\nexport const CONVEYOR_BELT_NPCS: ReadonlySet<number> = new Set([\n  624, 85, 629, 195, 1, 147, 184, 537, 204, 16, 59, 71, 535, 225, 676, 303,\n  335, 336, 333, 334, 667, 141, 81, 121, 183, 138, 244, 304, 105, 123, 685,\n  686, 687, 106, 354, 376, 579, 589, 37, 695, 696,\n]);\n\n/** NPC.cs:93780-93787 ConveyorBeltCollision() 门：城镇 NPC / 小动物\n *  （lifeMax==5 && damage==0 启发式 ≙ vanilla.critter）/ 上表敌怪才吃带。\n *  城镇 NPC 与小动物在 Game 接线处走各自桶（恒吃带），本函数只服务 enemies 桶。 */\nexport function conveyorCarriesEnemy(\n  e: { vanillaId?: number | null; vanilla?: { critter?: boolean } | null },\n): boolean {\n  if (e.vanilla?.critter) return true;\n  return e.vanillaId != null && CONVEYOR_BELT_NPCS.has(e.vanillaId);\n}\n\n/** 带面/带底两道扫描线的 Y（原版 :4277-4294 的 slope/halfBrick 几何，1:1 端点）。\n *  kind=2 上沿线（命中 +1）、kind=1 下沿线（命中 -1，:4295-4298）；t = 格内像素偏移。\n *  slope1=左高右低 2=右高左低 3/4=天花板坡（与 TileCollision.ts 坡面编码一致）。 */\nfunction beltLineY(kind: 1 | 2, slope: number, half: boolean, ty: number, t: number): number {\n  const top = ty * TILE, bot = ty * TILE + TILE;\n  switch (slope) {\n    case 1: return kind === 2 ? top + t : bot;              // :4279-4283\n    case 2: return kind === 2 ? bot - t : bot;              // :4284-4288\n    case 3: return kind === 2 ? top : bot - t;              // :4289-4293\n    case 4: return kind === 2 ? top : top + t;              // :4284-4293 的 4 分支\n    default: return kind === 2 ? top + (half ? 8 : 0) : bot; // :4295-4301 半砖顶 +8\n  }\n}\n\n/** AABB 与带线段相交（原版 CheckAABBvLineCollision2 :265 的本场景简化）：\n *  线段恒为全格宽且 X 单调，取实体 X 与格 X 的交集后比较两端 Y 区间即可。\n *  返回 [yMin, yMax]（交叠段的线段 Y 范围）或 null（无 X 交集）。 */\nfunction beltLineSpan(\n  ax0: number, ax1: number, tx: number,\n  kind: 1 | 2, slope: number, half: boolean, ty: number,\n): [number, number] | null {\n  const xs0 = Math.max(ax0, tx * TILE), xs1 = Math.min(ax1, tx * TILE + TILE);\n  if (xs1 < xs0) return null;\n  const ya = beltLineY(kind, slope, half, ty, xs0 - tx * TILE);\n  const yb = beltLineY(kind, slope, half, ty, xs1 - tx * TILE);\n  return ya <= yb ? [ya, yb] : [yb, ya];\n}\n\ninterface BeltContact {\n  /** Σ dir×num5×gravDir（:4304），Math.Sign 前的原值 */\n  num: number;\n  /** 坡面垂直分量 Σ（:4306-4310） */\n  num2: number;\n  /** 物品分档用的带向×重叠量 Σ（:3988-4002 的 Bottom/Top 接触） */\n  overlapX: number;\n}\n\n/** 脚下一行扫描（原版 GetEntityEdgeTiles :3882-3935 只取 down 边 + :4253 调用参数\n *  left:false right:false；底边恰在格顶时行号取带所在行 :3900-3902）。\n *  O(实体宽/16) 单点查表，不做全图扫描。 */\nfunction scanBeltContact(ent: ConveyorBody, st: TileStore, gravDir: number): BeltContact | null {\n  // 列范围：边界贴合时原版向两侧各扩一列（:3893-3899）\n  let l = Math.floor(ent.x), r = Math.floor(ent.x + ent.w);\n  if (l % TILE === 0) l--;\n  if (r % TILE === 0) r++;\n  const c0 = Math.floor(l / TILE), c1 = Math.floor(r / TILE);\n  // 行：底边恰在格顶（正常落脚）→ 取带所在行\n  let b = Math.floor(ent.y + ent.h);\n  if (b % TILE === 0) b++;\n  const row = Math.floor(b / TILE);\n\n  const ax0 = ent.x, ax1 = ent.x + ent.w, ay0 = ent.y, ay1 = ent.y + ent.h;\n  const contact: BeltContact = { num: 0, num2: 0, overlapX: 0 };\n  let found = false;\n  for (let c = c0; c <= c1; c++) {\n    if (!st.inBounds(c, row)) continue;\n    const j = st.idx(c, row);\n    const dir = conveyorDirection(st.type[j]);\n    if (dir === 0) continue;\n    // 原版要求 active() && nactive()（:4249-4252）：已致动（缩地）的带不搬运\n    if (!st.isActive(c, row)) continue;\n    const slope = st.slope[j], half = st.half[j] !== 0;\n    // num5：上沿线命中 +1、下沿线命中 -1（:4295-4301；带非 platform，恒减）\n    let num5 = 0;\n    const top2 = beltLineSpan(ax0, ax1, c, 2, slope, half, row);\n    if (top2 && top2[0] <= ay1 + EPS && top2[1] >= ay0 - EPS) num5++;\n    const bot1 = beltLineSpan(ax0, ax1, c, 1, slope, half, row);\n    if (bot1 && bot1[0] <= ay1 + EPS && bot1[1] >= ay0 - EPS) num5--;\n    if (num5 === 0) continue;\n    found = true;\n    contact.num += dir * num5 * gravDir;\n    // 坡面垂直分量（:4306-4310；leftSlope={2,4}/rightSlope={1,3}，Tile.cs:340-358）\n    if (slope === 2 || slope === 4) contact.num2 += gravDir * -dir;\n    if (slope === 1 || slope === 3) contact.num2 -= gravDir * -dir;\n    // 物品分档重叠量（BuildTileContacts :2225-2230：X 需严格相交 +0.5、下限 1；\n    //  num5>0=站立其上取 +dir，num5<0=带悬于其下取 -dir，对齐 :3988-4002）\n    const raw = Math.min(ax1, c * TILE + TILE) - Math.max(ax0, c * TILE);\n    if (raw > 0) contact.overlapX += dir * num5 * Math.max(raw + 0.5, 1);\n  }\n  return found ? contact : null;\n}\n\n/** 单轴平移 + tile 钳制（原版 TileCollision :2340 起，本场景 ≤2.5px 单步的等价简化；\n *  守卫与 physics/TileCollision.ts moveAxis 同款：贴面接近才拦、坡面格放行）。\n *  与 moveAxis 的区别：不写 vx/vy/onGround（原版 TileCollision 也只返回钳制后的位移）。 */\nfunction clampMove(ent: ConveyorBody, st: TileStore, dx: number, dy: number): void {\n  if (dx !== 0) {\n    const dir = dx > 0 ? 1 : -1;\n    const oldEdge = dir > 0 ? ent.x + ent.w : ent.x;\n    ent.x += dx;\n    const edge = dir > 0 ? ent.x + ent.w : ent.x;\n    const tx = Math.floor(edge / TILE);\n    const y0 = Math.floor(ent.y / TILE), y1 = Math.floor((ent.y + ent.h - 0.01) / TILE);\n    for (let ty = y0; ty <= y1; ty++) {\n      if (!st.inBounds(tx, ty)) continue;\n      const j = st.idx(tx, ty);\n      if (!st.isSolid(tx, ty) || st.slope[j] > 0) continue;\n      const top = ty * TILE + (st.half[j] ? 8 : 0);\n      if (ent.y + ent.h <= top) continue;\n      // 上一位置完全在该格一侧（贴面接近）才拦：已横跨的不拦（moveAxis :62 同款）\n      if (dir > 0 ? oldEdge > tx * TILE + 0.01 : oldEdge < (tx + 1) * TILE - 0.01) continue;\n      ent.x = dir > 0 ? tx * TILE - ent.w : (tx + 1) * TILE;\n      break;\n    }\n  }\n  if (dy !== 0) {\n    const dir = dy > 0 ? 1 : -1;\n    const prevBottom = ent.y + ent.h;\n    ent.y += dy;\n    const ty = Math.floor((dir > 0 ? ent.y + ent.h : ent.y) / TILE);\n    const x0 = Math.floor(ent.x / TILE), x1 = Math.floor((ent.x + ent.w - 0.01) / TILE);\n    let best = Infinity;\n    for (let tx = x0; tx <= x1; tx++) {\n      if (!st.inBounds(tx, ty)) continue;\n      const j = st.idx(tx, ty);\n      if (dir > 0) {\n        const solid = st.isSolid(tx, ty) && st.slope[j] === 0;\n        const top = solid ? ty * TILE + (st.half[j] ? 8 : 0)\n          : (!ent.dropThrough && st.isPlatform(tx, ty)) ? ty * TILE : -1;\n        if (top < 0) continue;\n        // 落地门槛（moveAxis :96-97 同款）：新底越过盒顶且上一位置在盒顶之上\n        if (ent.y + ent.h <= top || prevBottom > top + 0.01) continue;\n        if (top < best) best = top;\n      } else {\n        if (!st.isSolid(tx, ty) || st.slope[j] > 0) continue;\n        const bot = (ty + 1) * TILE;\n        if (ent.y >= bot || ent.y - dy < bot - 0.01) continue;\n        if (bot < best) best = bot;\n      }\n    }\n    if (best !== Infinity) ent.y = dir > 0 ? best - ent.h : best;\n  }\n}\n\n/** 掉落物档位（Collision.cs:4052-4077 1:1）：|Σ带向×重叠| ≤4→0.75、≤8→1.25、>8→2.5 */\nfunction itemTier(v: number): number {\n  if (v < 0) return v < -8 ? -CONVEYOR_ITEM_MAX : v < -4 ? -1.25 : -0.75;\n  if (v > 0) return v > 8 ? CONVEYOR_ITEM_MAX : v > 4 ? 1.25 : 0.75;\n  return 0;\n}\n\n/**\n * 传送带实体输送（Collision.StepConveyorBelt，Collision.cs:4215-4351 的脚下格简化版）。\n *\n * @param ent    被输送实体（Player/NPC/掉落物均可）\n * @param st     TileStore\n * @param gravDir 重力方向（Player 取 gravDir；NPC 原版恒 1f，NPC.cs:93778）\n * @param itemMode 掉落物路径（ApplyConveyorBeltMovementToVelocity :3936-4211）：\n *                 改 vx 逼近档位目标而非直接位移\n * @returns 是否接触传送带（原版 flag :4302，WorldItem.onConveyor 同义）\n */\nexport function stepConveyor(\n  ent: ConveyorBody, st: TileStore, gravDir = 1, itemMode = false,\n): boolean {\n  const contact = scanBeltContact(ent, st, gravDir);\n  if (!contact) return false;\n  if (itemMode) {\n    // 掉落物：vx 逼近档位目标，钳到目标（:4130-4140 1:1）\n    const target = itemTier(contact.overlapX);\n    if (target < 0 && ent.vx > target) ent.vx = Math.max(ent.vx + target * CONVEYOR_ITEM_ACCEL, target);\n    else if (target > 0 && ent.vx < target) ent.vx = Math.min(ent.vx + target * CONVEYOR_ITEM_ACCEL, target);\n    return true;\n  }\n  // Player/NPC：方向 Math.Sign 归一（:4328-4329）后 2.5px 步进（:4330-4334）\n  const num = Math.sign(contact.num);\n  if (num === 0) return true; // 双向带互相抵消（如跨 421|422 边界）：在带上但不动\n  const num2 = Math.sign(contact.num2);\n  const len = Math.hypot(num * gravDir, num2);\n  const dx = (num * gravDir / len) * CONVEYOR_SPEED;\n  const dy = (num2 / len) * CONVEYOR_SPEED;\n  clampMove(ent, st, dx, dy);\n  // 下压贴合（:4337-4341）：平移后向下探 2.5×gravDir，贴住带面/下坡不断联\n  clampMove(ent, st, 0, CONVEYOR_SPEED * gravDir);\n  return true;\n}\n"
}

Case 2: lineEnd2.Y = y*16; lineStart2.Y = bot → line2: (x*16, bot) → (x*16+16, top) = bot - t ✓; line1 bot ✓.

Good.

One issue in scanBeltContact: `const j = st.idx(c, row)` is computed before the isActive check — fine. But I compute `j` then use `st.slope[j]`, `st.half[j]` ✓.

Also note: for the row scan — an entity standing on a belt has bottom exactly at tileTop. beltLineSpan for kind=2 (top line horizontal at tileTop): xs range = entity∩tile; ya=yb=tileTop. Hit test: `top2[0] <= ay1 + EPS && top2[1] >= ay0 - EPS` → tileTop <= bottom+0.1 ✓ (equal) && tileTop >= top-0.1 ✓ → num5=+1 ✓. kind=1 (bot line at tileTop+16): 336 <= bottom+0.1=320.1? No → num5 stays 1 ✓.

For a half-brick belt: top line at +8. Entity resting on half-brick: bottom = tileTop+8 ✓ → +1 ✓.

Now, one thing I should double check: the down-row selection when the entity stands on a belt whose top is at row boundary: bottom=320 → b=320, 320%16==0 → b=321 → row=20 ✓ (belt row).

But what if bottom = 320.5 (float drift)? b = floor(320.5) = 320 → 320%16==0 → b=321 → row 20 ✓ same.

What if bottom = 321 (sunk 1px)? b=321 → 321%16=1 → row = floor(321/16)=20 ✓.

Edge: entity bottom = 336 exactly (sunk a full tile into the belt, i.e., inside the belt tile): b=336 → %16==0 → 337 → row 21 (the row BELOW the belt). Vanilla same ✓. And then contact for the belt row isn't scanned — vanilla also only scans the bottom edge row ✓. (The containsBottom→num5=-1 case only arises for entities whose bottom row IS the belt row but whose top is below the belt's top line — e.g., a 16px-tall entity sunk between tileTop+4 and tileTop+20? bottom=row*16+something... Let me verify my implementation can produce num5=-1: entity with bottom = tileTop+16-1 = 335, top = tileTop+4 (h=12... wait top=324,bottom=335? that's h=11, sunk 15px): row = floor(335/16)=20 ✓ belt row. kind2 line at 320: 320 <= 335+0.1 ✓ && 320 >= 324-0.1=323.9? NO → top line not hit. kind1 line at 336: 336 <= 335.1? No → not hit either → num5=0 → skipped. Hmm — vanilla: AABB [324,335]; line1 at 336 not contained → also no. For num5=-1 need AABB to contain 336 but not 320: bottom ≥ 336 → but then row = 21 → different row, belt not scanned. So in flat-belt practice num5=-1 only matters for slopes. Fine — the math is there for slopes.)

Now — one important behavior check for the player path: after clampMove(ent, st, dx, dy) with dx=±2.5: the X clamp's y-range scan is y0..y1 over the entity's body. A player standing on the belt: body rows above the belt row; the wall check hits a solid tile in those rows ✓.

But wait: the X clamp scans rows from floor(ent.y/16) to floor((ent.y+ent.h-0.01)/16) — for a player standing ON the belt, the bottom row (belt row) is NOT included (bottom-0.01 is above the belt top) — correct: the belt itself shouldn't block.

However there's a subtle problem: the X clamp would block on the belt tile itself if the entity's bottom sank into the belt row (e.g., after glue... no). Fine.

Another important check: the glue step clampMove(ent, st, 0, 2.5): prevBottom = bottom (== tileTop after horizontal move... the horizontal move doesn't change y) → the target row = floor((tileTop+2.5)/16) = belt row → belt is solid, slope 0 → top = tileTop → `ent.y + ent.h <= top`? bottom = tileTop+2.5 > tileTop → no skip; `prevBottom > top + 0.01`? tileTop > tileTop+0.01? No → best = tileTop → ent.y = tileTop - h → glued ✓.

Now — a real gameplay concern: the glue step uses platforms too (st.isPlatform with prevBottom <= ty*TILE+1). When carried off the belt edge over a platform 1 tile below, glue snaps at most 2.5px down — negligible, fine.

                // 带所在行，顶面 y = 320\nconst SURFACE = ROW * 16;\n\nfunction body(x: number, y: number, w = 20, h = 42, vx = 0) {\n  return { x, y, w, h, vx, vy: 0 };\n}\n\n/** 在 ROW 行铺 cols 范围的带 */\nfunction layBelt(st: TileStore, c0: number, c1: number, type: number) {\n  for (let c = c0; c <= c1; c++) st.setTileSilent(c, ROW, type);\n}\n\ndescribe('传送带实体输送（StepConveyorBelt 1:1）', () => {\n  it('421（Clockwise）：站立每 tick 向 +X 平移 2.5px，vy/位置吸附不变', () => {\n    const w = new World(60, 40, 42);\n    layBelt(w.store, 10, 19, BELT_421);\n    const b = body(10 * 16, SURFACE - 42);\n    expect(stepConveyor(b, w.store)).toBe(true);\n    expect(b.x).toBeCloseTo(10 * 16 + CONVEYOR_SPEED);   // :4333 的 2.5f\n    expect(b.y).toBe(SURFACE - 42);                       // 平带下压贴合无位移(:4337-4141)\n    for (let i = 1; i < 10; i++) expect(stepConveyor(b, w.store)).toBe(true);\n    expect(b.x).toBeCloseTo(10 * 16 + 10 * CONVEYOR_SPEED); // 恒速 2.5/tick\n  });\n\n  it('422（Counter Clockwise）：向 -X 平移 2.5px', () => {\n    const w = new World(60, 40, 42);\n    layBelt(w.store, 10, 19, BELT_422);\n    const b = body(15 * 16, SURFACE - 42);\n    expect(stepConveyor(b, w.store)).toBe(true);\n    expect(b.x).toBeCloseTo(15 * 16 - CONVEYOR_SPEED);\n  });\n\n  it('非带上（石地/平台/悬空）不动且返回 false', () => {\n    const w = new World(60, 40, 42);\n    for (let c = 10; c <= 19; c++) w.store.setTileSilent(c, ROW, STONE);\n    const onStone = body(10 * 16, SURFACE - 42);\n    expect(stepConveyor(onStone, w.store)).toBe(false);\n    expect(onStone.x).toBe(10 * 16);\n\n    const w2 = new World(60, 40, 42);\n    for (let c = 10; c <= 19; c++) w2.store.setTileSilent(c, ROW, PLATFORM);\n    const onPlat = body(10 * 16, SURFACE - 42);\n    expect(stepConveyor(onPlat, w2.store)).toBe(false);\n    expect(onPlat.x).toBe(10 * 16);\n\n    const w3 = new World(60, 40, 42);\n    layBelt(w3.store, 10, 19, BELT_421);\n    const air = body(10 * 16, SURFACE - 42 - 4); // 悬空 4px：未接触带面\n    expect(stepConveyor(air, w3.store)).toBe(false);\n    expect(air.x).toBe(10 * 16);\n  });\n\n  it('掉落物：vx 按 0.1/tick 逼近档位目标，钳 2.5 不超限（WorldItem 路径）', () => {\n    const w = new World(60, 40, 42);\n    layBelt(w.store, 10, 19, BELT_421);\n    const item = body(12 * 16, SURFACE - 12, 12, 12); // 12px 全在带内 → 档位 2.5(:4068)\n    expect(stepConveyor(item, w.store, 1, true)).toBe(true);\n    expect(item.vx).toBeCloseTo(0.25);                 // 2.5×6/60(:4131)\n    expect(item.x).toBe(12 * 16);                      // item 模式只改 vx 不位移\n    for (let i = 0; i < 200; i++) {\n      stepConveyor(item, w.store, 1, true);\n      expect(item.vx).toBeLessThanOrEqual(CONVEYOR_ITEM_MAX + 1e-9);\n    }\n    expect(item.vx).toBeCloseTo(CONVEYOR_ITEM_MAX, 5); // 收敛到上限\n  });\n\n  it('换向（Wiring.cs:1020-1027 翻 421↔422）后方向反转', () => {\n    const w = new World(60, 40, 42);\n    layBelt(w.store, 10, 19, BELT_421);\n    const b = body(14 * 16, SURFACE - 42);\n    stepConveyor(b, w.store);\n    expect(b.x).toBeCloseTo(14 * 16 + CONVEYOR_SPEED);\n    // devices.ts case 421 的同款翻转：type 互换、帧保留\n    for (let c = 10; c <= 19; c++) w.store.setTileSilent(c, ROW, BELT_422);\n    stepConveyor(b, w.store);\n    expect(b.x).toBeCloseTo(14 * 16);                  // +2.5 后 -2.5 回原位\n  });\n\n  it('上限/抵消：跨 421|422 边界双向抵消不动；实体自带 vx 不放大步长', () => {\n    const w = new World(60, 40, 42);\n    w.store.setTileSilent(12, ROW, BELT_421);\n    w.store.setTileSilent(13, ROW, BELT_422);\n    const straddle = body(12 * 16 + 8, SURFACE - 42);  // 骑在边界上\n    expect(stepConveyor(straddle, w.store)).toBe(true); // 在带上（flag）\n    expect(straddle.x).toBe(12 * 16 + 8);              // num=0 → 不动(:4330 num!=0 门)\n\n    const w2 = new World(60, 40, 42);\n    layBelt(w2.store, 10, 19, BELT_421);\n    const fast = body(10 * 16, SURFACE - 42, 20, 42, 10); // 自带 vx=10\n    stepConveyor(fast, w2.store);\n    expect(fast.x).toBeCloseTo(10 * 16 + CONVEYOR_SPEED); // 步进恒 2.5，与 vx 无关\n    expect(fast.vx).toBe(10);                             // 原版不改 velocity\n  });\n\n  it('致动（inActive）的带不搬运（:4249-4252 nactive 门）', () => {\n    const w = new World(60, 40, 42);\n    layBelt(w.store, 10, 19, BELT_421);\n    const i = w.store.idx(12, ROW);\n    w.store.wire[i] |= TileStore.WIRE_ACTUATED;\n    const b = body(11 * 16, SURFACE - 42);\n    expect(stepConveyor(b, w.store)).toBe(false);\n    expect(b.x).toBe(11 * 16);\n  });\n\n  it('半砖带：顶面 +8 仍接触并平移', () => {\n    const w = new World(60, 40, 42);\n    layBelt(w.store, 10, 19, BELT_421);\n    const i = w.store.idx(12, ROW);\n    w.store.half[i] = 1;\n    const b = body(12 * 16 - 2, SURFACE - 8 - 42 + 42 - 42 + 34, 20, 42);\n    // 直接站半砖顶：底 = SURFACE+8 → y = SURFACE+8-42\n    b.y = SURFACE + 8 - 42;\n    expect(stepConveyor(b, w.store)).toBe(true);\n    expect(b.x).toBeCloseTo(12 * 16 - 2 + CONVEYOR_SPEED);\n  });\n\n  it('带推向墙壁：步进被钳制在墙面，不嵌入（:4335 TileCollision 钳制）', () => {\n    const w = new World(60, 40, 42);\n    layBelt(w.store, 10, 14, BELT_421);\n    for (let r = 15; r <= 19; r++) w.store.setTileSilent(15, r, STONE); // 带尽头的墙\n    const b = body(15 * 16 - 20 - 2, SURFACE - 42); // 距墙 2px\n    expect(stepConveyor(b, w.store)).toBe(true);\n    expect(b.x).toBe(15 * 16 - 20);                 // 只吃到 2px，贴墙停住\n    expect(b.x + b.w).toBe(15 * 16);\n  });\n\n  it('掉落物档位：带缘小重叠走 0.75 低档（:4052-4077）', () => {\n    const w = new World(60, 40, 42);\n    w.store.setTileSilent(10, ROW, BELT_421); // 单格带\n    const item = body(10 * 16 + 13, SURFACE - 12, 12, 12); // 只压带 3px\n    stepConveyor(item, w.store, 1, true);\n    expect(item.vx).toBeCloseTo(0.75 * 0.1); // 档位 0.75 → 首 tick +0.075\n  });\n\n  it('敌怪门（NPC.cs:93780-93787 + NPCID.cs:4789）：普通敌怪不吃带、表内/小动物吃', () => {\n    expect(conveyorCarriesEnemy({ vanillaId: 3, vanilla: { critter: false } })).toBe(false); // 僵尸\n    expect(conveyorCarriesEnemy({ vanillaId: 1, vanilla: { critter: false } })).toBe(true);  // 表内\n    expect(conveyorCarriesEnemy({ vanillaId: 539, vanilla: null })).toBe(true);\n    expect(conveyorCarriesEnemy({ vanillaId: null, vanilla: { critter: true } })).toBe(true); // 小动物\n  });\n});\n"
}

完成。以下为最终报告。

## 改动文件
- **新增** `~/Project/GLM/SandboxWorld/game/src/physics/Conveyor.ts` — `stepConveyor(ent, st, gravDir=1, itemMode=false): boolean` 纯函数 + `conveyorCarriesEnemy`/`conveyorDirection`/`CONVEYOR_BELT_NPCS`
- **改动** `~/Project/GLM/SandboxWorld/game/src/core/Game.ts` — 两处：import（+1 行）、`fixedUpdate` 在 `this.entities.update(...)` 后插入统一后处理块（player/npcs/critters/drops 各一行、enemies 带门一行）。对各自 moveAndCollide 零侵入，`devices.ts` case 421/422 未动
- **新增** `~/Project/GLM/SandboxWorld/game/tests/conveyor.test.ts` — 11 例

## 关键调研结论：方向与任务线索相反（以反编译源码为准）
任务线索写"421 向左/422 向右"，但 1456 源码两条独立链路一致给出**反号**：
- `Collision.cs:4333`：`velocity = Normalize((num*gravDir, num2)) * 2.5`，其中 `num = Σ ConveyorDirection×num5×gravDir`（:4304），站立时 num5=+1（上沿线命中 :4299-4301）→ **421（ConveyorDirection=+1，TileID.cs:261）向 +X（右）、422 向 -X（左）**
- 交叉印证：掉落物链路 `BuildTileContacts`（:2212-2216）的 `Side=Bottom`（物品底==格顶）→ `zero.X += +num3×overlap`（:4001）同号
- 素材键 `v_421_conveyorbeltleft` 的 left/right 是命名误导；物品名实为 "Conveyor Belt (Clockwise / Counter Clockwise)"（idNames.generated.ts:11361-11362）。测试按源码方向断言

## 数值（1456 最终态）
- **Player/NPC**（`StepConveyorBelt` Collision.cs:4215-4351）：固定步进 **2.5px/tick**，方向 `Math.Sign` 归一（:4328-4329），直接改 position 不改 velocity；先经 TileCollision 钳制平移（:4335-4336，本作内联 `clampMove` 等价，撞墙贴面停），再向下压 2.5×gravDir 贴合带面（:4337-4341，平带为 no-op、下坡/跨带不断联）；调用点 Player.cs:27920（矿车变体 :23294）、NPC.cs:93774（gravDir 恒 1f）
- **掉落物**（`ApplyConveyorBeltMovementToVelocity` Collision.cs:3936-4211，WorldItem.cs:938）：速度驱动，按带向×重叠量分档 **|Σ|≤4→0.75 / ≤8→1.25 / >8→2.5**（:4052-4077），每 tick `vx += 目标×6/60`（=0.1）且钳到目标（:4130-4140）→ 加速逼近、上限 2.5
- **致动**：`active() && nactive()`（:4249-4252）→ 已致动带不搬运（用 `st.isActive`）；**半砖**顶面 +8 仍接触（:4295-4301）；**坡面**几何与垂直分量 1:1（:4277-4310，leftSlope={2,4}/rightSlope={1,3}，Tile.cs:340-358）
- **敌怪门**（NPC.cs:93780-93787）：`townNPC || (lifeMax==5&&damage==0) || NPCID.Sets.ConveyorBeltCollision`——即**普通敌怪原版不吃带**，仅表内 41 种（NPCID.cs:4789）+ 小动物 + 城镇 NPC；已按此实现
- **换向**：原版是 `tile.type` 421↔422 互换（Wiring.cs:1020-1027），非 frameX 翻转；我方 devices.ts 已同款，输送方向自动跟随 tile 类型

## 备案（未移植，均已在文件头注释）
时序后处理化（原版在碰撞段内，每 tick 末等价）；接触容差统一 0.1px；Player gfxOffY/抓钩/pulley/onTrack 门与 height-5 收缩（本作无对应状态）；物品侧向接触 Y 速度与带端抬升快照（:3984-4020、:4102-4128）；artificialRising 人工爬升（`TryFindingConveyorBeltRising` :4353 在 1.4.5.6 **无调用方=死代码**）；`onConveyor` 摆动视觉标志（WorldItem.cs:41、Main.cs:38049）。
